If a join lists `metadata_schema` among its ingredients, a missing schema or an unreadable file stops the build with an error that names the dataset." and "Polars promises no row order unless it is asked for. The actions that can reorder rows (group by, sort, join, unique) need their order parameter in the step (`maintain_order`, `keep`), and sample needs a `seed`. Actions that work by position (head, tail, slice, first, last, row index) use the order of the table at their step, so write a `polars_sort` with `maintain_order: true` before them. When rows tie, `top_k` and `bottom_k` do not promise which tied rows are kept, or in what order. The same input then gives the same rows in the same order on every run (ADR-090)."

## Decisions for Eve collected in this session (new; the older ones are in the handoff of 2026-11-14 night, points 1 to 5)
N1. Join key rule in three places: the build (`DataAssembler._check_join_key_types`), the Blueprint preview (`check_join_keys`), the checker (`join_key_problems`). Kept in step by a parity test only. Options: keep the copies with the parity tests; or one pure rule in `libs/utils` called by all three.
N2. Blueprint join preview: `except Exception` in `_jd_run_preview` shows any programming error to the user as "Preview failed: ...". Options: leave; or catch only expected errors and let programming errors propagate.
N3. Gallery with a read-only folder: the start check cannot rewrite the index (status `not_writable`), so a failing example listed in the old index is still shown; the only trace is a console warning. Options: keep; or leave failing examples out in memory in the handlers (`bootloader.rejected_gallery_examples`).
N4. Gallery: what the user sees for an example left out at start is not specified (spec section 9). Today: left out of the index, reason in `metadata.rejected` and a console warning; no screen element.
N5. Checker J10 (a join key not declared in `input_fields` is refused at load): confirm. It mirrors the build because the ingestor imports only the declared columns when `input_fields` is not empty.
N6. AUDIT-REPAIR-1: for four old words (`persona`, `manifest`, `profile`, `project`) and one UML tag: fix the text or adapt the exclusions (not recorded which); `recipe` waits for RECIPE-WORD-1.
N7. Tier-3 reader (W10): needs HOME's P5 mechanism spec and the order of change R1 (`recipe_format_spec.md` line 173); `beeswarm_sina` waits for it.
N8. W14 Blueprint package: tasks.md line 82 says Blueprint is not touched now, the handoff listed it as next. Eve 2026-11-14: Blueprint and Test Lab can wait.

N9. (from HOME-EXPLORE-1) VizFactory `in` / `not_in` compares as text, so on a numeric column `in [90.0]` or `in [90.5, 91]` gives no rows in the PLOT while the HOME table keeps them.
